# Count continuation — bar-by-bar causal replay

_Generated 2026-11-06 by `scripts/count_history.py`. At each step the engine saw only the bars up to that point; every row is a labelling that was actually held, including those later abandoned. Analysis tooling only — not investment advice._

## AVGO — Broadcom · NASDAQ:AVGO
- 4340 1d bars 2009-08-06 → 2026-11-05, snapshot `2026-11`; interactive page: `charts/avgo_count_history_2026-11.html`

## AVGO — plain-language read as of 2026-11-05 ($291.53)

**Stance: CONFIRMED BIAS.** DOWN bias, confirmed on 2026-11-05 by the break below $356.43. Target $208.52; the read is wrong above $432.73; expect resolution by about 2027-01-30. Only 2/7 independent strands agree — low conviction.

**Where we are**
- On the last 300 daily bars the engine's best read is a ZIGZAG whose final wave C is STILL RUNNING (Minor degree, 42% confidence — low numbers are normal here; Elliott counts are interpretive).
- Its legs: A ended 2026-07-02 at $356.43, B ended 2026-08-10 at $432.73; wave C running since 2026-08-10 ($432.73), now $290.12.
- Price now: $291.53 (2026-11-05).

**What the engine expects next**
- Expected next: wave C completion (down) — i.e. a move DOWN.
- Target: $208.52; the strongest Fibonacci overlap is $208.52.
- Targets are measured from the confirmed pivots, never from today's price; only the levels still ahead of price are listed (the count was adopted 2026-11-04).
- Time: the move should complete within ~10-87 days of 2026-11-04 (by about 2027-01-30), measured against the prior 87-day wave.

**What confirms it / what kills it**
- Confirmation: a daily close below $356.43 (the end of the previous wave in this direction) proves the running wave is genuine. Until then this is only a bias.
- Price is already 18% below the trigger $356.43, so the expected move is in progress; 28% is left to the first target.
- Proven wrong: a move above $432.73 voids the count — the expectation is cancelled.
- Independent confirmation strands at the target zone right now: 2/7 (momentum divergence, MACD turn, volume climax, channel break, change of character, zone, cycle). Fewer than 4 means the label is NOT yet backed by price behaviour.
- From here: 28% to the first target versus 48% to the void level — reward-to-risk 0.6 to 1.

**How reliable is this kind of call**
- A decent track record: in this symbol's history, projections made from a completed zigzag reached their first target 60% of the time (81 of 134 decided), were proven wrong 36% of the time, typically taking ~13 bars to get there.

**What to do**
- Treat the structure as leaning DOWN toward $208.52.
- The read is cancelled above $432.73.
- If nothing happens by 2027-01-30, drop the read.

## MRVL — plain-language read as of 2026-11-05 ($133.55)

**Stance: PLAYED OUT.** Price ($133.55) has already moved past every level this count projected. The projection is exhausted — do not chase; wait for the next count to form.

**Where we are**
- On the last 300 daily bars the engine's best read is a completed IMPULSE (Minor degree) with 49% confidence — low numbers are normal here; Elliott counts are interpretive.
- Its legs: 1 ended 2025-11-06 at $98.13, 2 ended 2026-02-05 at $70.69, 3 ended 2026-06-03 at $324.20, 4 ended 2026-07-29 at $162.90, 5 ended 2026-10-06 at $301.27.
- Price now: $133.55 (2026-11-05).

**What the engine expects next**
- Expected next: corrective A-B-C (down) — i.e. a move DOWN.
- No target in the expected direction from here (price already past them).
- Targets are measured from the confirmed pivots, never from today's price; only the levels still ahead of price are listed (the count was adopted 2026-10-15).
- Time: the move should complete within ~23-207 days of 2026-10-15 (by about 2027-05-10), measured against the prior 69-day wave — a long window, so this is a slow, position-scale read, not a short-term call.

**What confirms it / what kills it**
- Confirmation: a daily close below $162.90 (the pivot before the last leg). Until then this is only a bias.
- Price is already 18% below the trigger $162.90, so the expected move is in progress; it has already run past every projected target.
- Proven wrong: a move below $61.44 voids the count — the expectation is cancelled.
- Nearer protective reference: just above the last pivot, $304.28.
- Independent confirmation strands at the target zone right now: 2/7 (momentum divergence, MACD turn, volume climax, channel break, change of character, zone, cycle). Fewer than 4 means the label is NOT yet backed by price behaviour.

**How reliable is this kind of call**
- A decent track record: in this symbol's history, projections made from a completed impulse reached their first target 54% of the time (54 of 100 decided), were proven wrong 22% of the time, typically taking ~8 bars to get there.

**What to do**
- Do nothing on this read; the move it called has happened.
- A fresh count will form once price makes a new confirmed swing; check back then.

### Glossary

- **Count** — the engine's labelling of recent swings as Elliott waves (1-5 motive, A-B-C corrective, W-X-Y double correction, A-E triangle).
- **Anchored count** — the committed count on the trailing 300 bars; 'recent candidate' is the cleanest small fragment, often older and less useful.
- **Confidence** — how well the structure obeys the rules and Fibonacci proportions; 20-50% is normal, it is not a probability of success.
- **Projection / forecast** — what should come next IF the count is right: direction, target levels, a void level and a time window.
- **T1 / far** — the nearest and farthest projected target in the expected direction.
- **Void (invalidation)** — the price that proves the count wrong; crossing it cancels the expectation.
- **Trigger (confirmation)** — the pivot before the last leg; a close beyond it is the first evidence the expected move has started. Never act on the label alone.
- **Time window** — NeoWave rule of thumb: the next wave takes between one third and three times the prior wave's duration.
- **Confluence x/7** — independent confirmation strands (momentum divergence, MACD turn, volume climax, channel break, change of character, zone, cycle). Below 4 = unconfirmed.
- **EXTEND / RELABEL / SHIFT** — how a held count changed: same origin with legs advanced / a different pattern / same pattern re-anchored elsewhere.
- **HIT / HIT_FULL / INVALIDATED / EXPIRED** — how a projection ended: nearest target reached / farthest reached / void crossed first / nothing within the time window.
